reviewer_name: Terri Head
rating: 5
body: We had reservations for our Valentine’s Day dinner at 5:00. It was our first time to eat here. We were sat immediately and by the windows for a beautiful view of the bay. Our server was Eian. He and the other dining room workers were great. He was very knowledgeable about the dinner menu and our glasses were never empty.  We started with the lobster rangoon as an appetizer. My husband had the bayou grouper and I had the stuffed shrimp as entrees. All three dishes were excellent. I would recommend this restaurant to anyone who loves seafood with a Cajun flair.
